Raise the abort to DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS or at least the bridge's 60s, and make summarizeWithHaiku rethrow (or have the timer treat "" as failure). Add a test for 'skip then meaningful turn arrives before next tick'.
- [high/never-retrieved] **DB mid-session flush re-summarizes the entire session every 15 minutes, and the rows it writes are never read, deduped, or pruned**
  Evidence: src/memory/session-summarizer.ts:524 `const turns = deps.conversationStore.getTurnsForSession(sessionId);` (no delta, no limit) and 601-623 insert with `skipDedup: true`. A session spans the whole agent uptime (session-manager.ts:1298 one `startSession` per startAgent). Readers exclude the tag: session-manager.ts:862-863 default excludeTags includes "mid-session"; src/memory/conversation-brief.ts:176 `findByTag("session-summary")` only; src/manager/memory-digest.ts:25-30 DIGEST_EXCLUDE_TAGS includes "mid-session"; src/memory/summary-dedupe.ts:136 `store.findByTag("session-summary")` only. No pruning path touches them.
  Impact: 96 Haiku calls and 96 embedded memory rows per agent per day of near-identical content (each a lossy 30k-char view of an ever-longer session), reachable only through the explicit search tool without excludeTags, where they crowd out real hits. Unbounded growth of memories/vec tables with zero recall benefit.
  Fix: Either set the flush to incremental (pass turns since the last flush, like memory-flush.ts, and have summarizeSession consume the flush chain plus the tail instead of all turns), or disable it by default (flushIntervalMinutes: 0) since the disk flush plus scanner already provides retrievable mid-session memory. Add "mid-session" to summary-dedupe's tag set either way.
- [high/never-injected] **Automatic compaction and the resume Context Summary are dead: nothing feeds the fill provider, and the compaction summary is thrown away**
  Evidence: src/memory/compaction.ts:192-194 `addTurn(content)` is the only input to CharacterCountFillProvider; repo-wide grep finds no `.addTurn(` caller, so src/heartbeat/checks/context-fill.ts:38-46 always reads 0 (src/manager/session-log-flusher.ts:12-15 documents the symptom: "SDK-session fleet reports ~0% daemon-side fill ... that compaction never fires"). `saveContextSummary` has a single caller, the zone snapshot at src/manager/daemon.ts:5590-5594 with text `Auto-snapshot at ${pct}% context fill [${zone} zone]`, which never fires at 0% fill. Meanwhile compaction.ts:157-171 builds `formattedSummary` ("Key context from previous session: ...") and src/manager/daemon-compact-session-ipc.ts:115 reduces it to `const summaryWritten = compactionResult.summary.length > 0;`. src/manager/session-config.ts:1085-1103 injects whatever context-summary.md holds.
  Impact: The 'Context Summary (from previous session)' prompt block is never populated in production, and the automatic Layer-1 compaction (the only automatic writer of extracted facts and tier2 active-state) never runs; only manual compact-session IPC does. The 1500-token resumeSummaryBudget machinery guards an empty slot.
  Fix: In handleCompactSession after Layer 1, call `manager.saveContextSummary(agent, compactionResult.summary)` when non-empty (and never overwrite it with the zone placeholder; write that elsewhere). Feed the fill provider from the real SDK token count (`getContextUsage`) or from captureDiscordExchange, or gate the auto-trigger on getContextUsage directly.
- [medium/design-gap] **Only Discord turns enter ConversationStore, so every automatic capture path is blind to other origins and to tool activity**
  Evidence: src/discord/capture.ts:66 `captureDiscordExchange` is the sole caller of `ConversationStore.recordTurn` (grep: capture.ts:87, 99 only); src/manager/turn-dispatcher.ts contains no recordTurn or convStore reference. Recorded assistant content is the final text (`assistantContent: response` at bridge.ts:6243, `parsed.text` at 2821/8226), so the tool marker check at src/memory/memory-flush.ts:111-112 `content.includes("tool_use") || content.includes("[tool use")` can never be true, and src/memory/sdk-transcript-reader.ts:5-13 confirms tool markers 'never appear' in ConversationStore turns.
  Impact: Scheduled-task runs, OpenAI-endpoint conversations, heartbeat prompts, cross-agent asks and workflow results produce no session summary, no flush, no daily log and no consolidation input; the disk flush's 'did work' heuristic degenerates to the 200-char rule.
  Fix: Record user/assistant turns in TurnDispatcher.dispatch for every TurnOrigin (RecordTurnInput already has an `origin` field, conversation-store.ts:506), and optionally append a compact `[tool use: name]` marker list to the assistant content from the attributed stream so the heuristic and extractors see tool activity.
- [medium/data-loss] **summarizeSession prunes raw turns even when the archive write failed**
  Evidence: src/memory/session-summarizer.ts:386-390 comment: "Archive failure also does not block the prune"; 391-412 catches `archiveSession` errors and only warns; 414-415 `const deleted = deps.conversationStore.deleteTurnsForSession(sessionId);` runs unconditionally after markSummarized. The summary that survives can be a proportionally truncated prompt (69-82: `maxPerTurn = MAX_PROMPT_CHARS / turns.length`).
  Impact: If session_archive insert fails (disk full, schema drift, bare DB), the only remaining record of the session is a lossy summary; raw turns are gone with no reconcile path.
  Fix: Track `archived` success and skip `deleteTurnsForSession` when the archive threw (leave turns for the next drain); tag the summary `archive-failed` so the doctor can retry.
- [medium/design-gap] **Session-boundary prompt truncation is proportional per turn, so long-lived sessions summarize almost nothing**
  Evidence: src/memory/session-summarizer.ts:71-82: `const maxPerTurn = turns.length > 0 ? Math.floor(MAX_PROMPT_CHARS / turns.length) : MAX_PROMPT_CHARS;` then `content.slice(0, maxPerTurn)`. A ConversationStore session lasts the whole agent uptime (session-manager.ts:1298), and flushSessionMidway uses the same prompt builder (541).
  Impact: A 1000-turn session yields 30 characters per turn; the final session-summary (the only thing the resume brief reads, conversation-brief.ts:176) and every mid-session flush carry no usable decisions or open threads.
  Fix: Keep the last N turns verbatim and feed earlier context from the previous flush summaries (chain summarization), or summarize in fixed windows and merge; at minimum drop whole oldest turns instead of shrinking all turns uniformly.
- [medium/design-gap] **Disk-flush and subagent files expire from retrieval after 14 days and are never consolidated**
  Evidence: src/memory/memory-chunks.ts:197-207 `isStandingMemoryPath` covers vault/, procedures/, digests/, document: and the remember-cue pattern only; session-manager.ts:938 `timeWindowDays: 14`; src/memory/consolidation.ts:136 `const datePattern = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\.md$/;` so YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM.md flushes never enter weekly digests. Node check: the cue regex does not match `/memory/2026-10-25-1430.md`.
  Impact: Decisions captured mid-session are forgotten after two weeks unless the explicit search tool is used; nothing folds them into the digests that are supposed to represent older context.
  Fix: Include `YYYY-MM-DD-*.md` flush and subagent files in detectUnconsolidatedWeeks input (and archive them with the daily logs), or treat them as standing until a digest covering their date exists.
- [medium/performance] **Scanner re-embeds the entire daily log on every heartbeat append and drops events that arrive mid-index**
  Evidence: src/memory/memory-scanner.ts:223-249 deletes every chunk for the path then `await this.deps.embed(textToEmbed)` for each chunk; src/memory/session-log.ts:83 `appendFile(filePath, entry)` runs on every heartbeat tick with new turns (session-log-flush check, 60s default), and chunkMarkdownByH2 turns each `## HH:MM:SS [role]` header into a chunk. memory-scanner.ts:205 `if (this.indexing.has(absPath)) return 0;` discards a concurrent change instead of queuing it.
  Impact: A busy day costs O(turns) MiniLM embeds per tick; a second write landing during a long re-index is not indexed until some later unrelated change, so retrieval lags the daily log.
  Fix: Diff by (chunkIndex, heading, body) hash against existing memory_chunks rows and embed only new/changed chunks; replace the `indexing` short-circuit with a 'rerun after current' flag.
- [medium/inert-config] **memoryCueEmoji is parsed and resolved but never read; the reaction is hardcoded**
  Evidence: src/config/schema.ts:2674 and 3470 define `memoryCueEmoji`; src/config/loader.ts:1310 resolves it; src/shared/types.ts:450 carries it; daemon.ts:9703 lists it as hot-reloadable; src/manager/turn-dispatcher.ts:1443 `void reactor({ channelId, messageId }, "✅")`.
  Impact: Operators who set a custom cue emoji see no change, and the hot-reload entry is a no-op.
  Fix: Thread `sessionManager.getAgentConfig(agentName)?.memoryCueEmoji ?? "✅"` into the reactor call.
- [low/bug] **Scanner's subagent exclusion regex never matches the files subagent-capture writes**
  Evidence: src/memory/memory-scanner.ts:47 `if (/\/memory\/subagent-/.test(absPath)) return false;` versus src/memory/subagent-capture.ts:99 `${date}-subagent-${baseSlug}.md`. Node check: the regex returns false for `/ws/memory/2026-10-25-subagent-research-x.md`. No scanner test covers subagent paths (memory-scanner.test.ts:52-118).
  Impact: Documented intent (exclude) and behaviour (index) disagree; moot today because capture never fires, but once MEM-06 is wired the outcome is accidental rather than chosen.
  Fix: Decide explicitly: delete the exclusion (indexing subagent reports is the useful behaviour) and update the header comment, or change the regex to `/\/memory\/\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-subagent-/`; add a test either way.
- [low/bug] **Cue regex fires on any 'remember', writing permanent standing memory files for recall questions**
  Evidence: src/memory/memory-cue.ts:44-45 `/(remember( this)?|...)/i`; node check: matches "I don't remember the meeting" and "do you remember what we decided?". src/memory/__tests__/memory-cue.test.ts:53-64 explicitly accepts the false positive. Files are standing forever (src/memory/memory-chunks.ts:202-207) and there is no dedup between cue files.
  Impact: Every recall question becomes an undeletable, always-retrieved 'Standing note' containing a question rather than a rule, polluting the standing-memory slot that outranks dated files.
  Fix: Require an imperative/declarative form (`\bremember (this|that|to|:)`, `keep this in memory`, etc.) and reject `don't|do you|can't|didn't remember`; skip the write when an identical context already exists for the day.
- [low/data-loss] **Daily-log flusher reads only the active session, so turns between the last tick and a restart never reach the daily log**
  Evidence: src/manager/session-log-flusher.ts:98-100 `deps.getTurns(deps.activeSessionId)`; on restart a fresh session is started and the old one is closed as crashed (session-manager.ts:3064-3071, conversation-store.ts:223-233) and never consulted by the flusher. The cursor is global per memoryDir (session-log-flusher.ts:62-79) and is written with plain writeFileSync.
  Impact: Up to one heartbeat interval of turns per restart is missing from YYYY-MM-DD.md, hence from consolidation digests and scanner chunks (they survive only in session_archive after summarization).
  Fix: On the first tick after boot, also flush turns from sessions closed by closeStaleActiveSessions whose createdAt is newer than the cursor; write the cursor via atomicWriteFile.
- [low/silent-failure] **Skip reasons on embed/insert failure are mislabelled**
  Evidence: src/memory/session-summarizer.ts:307-311 and 346-350 return `reason: "session-not-terminal"` for embedder and insert failures; 590-594 and 635-639 return `reason: "zero-turns"` for the same failures in flushSessionMidway.
  Impact: Dashboards and the summarize-pending counters cannot distinguish an infrastructure failure from a legitimately empty or active session, so retry backlogs look healthy.
  Fix: Add `"embed-failed"` and `"insert-failed"` to the skipped-reason unions and return them.
- [low/never-retrieved] **events.jsonl and the session_logs table are written but never consumed, and grow without bound**
  Evidence: src/memory/session-log.ts:45-63 appends stall rows to `events.jsonl` 'for Phase 124/125 compaction extractors'; grep over src/manager/compact-extractors finds no 'stall' or 'events.jsonl' reader (only the writer in src/manager/stream-stall-callback.ts:163). src/memory/compaction.ts:132 `memoryStore.recordSessionLog(...)` inserts a row per compaction; src/manager/session-log-flusher.ts:21-25 states consolidation detects logs by readdir, and store.ts:654/675 are the only session_logs statements.
  Impact: Operator-visible stall patterns promised in the docstring never surface; two append-only sinks accrue indefinitely.
  Fix: Either render stall counts into the active-state header as intended, or drop recordStall and the session_logs insert.
- [low/race] **Disk-flush filename has minute granularity, so two flushes in one minute overwrite each other**
  Evidence: src/memory/memory-flush.ts:260-267 builds `${date}-${hhmm}.md` from the flush start time and atomicWriteFile renames over the destination (141). A periodic tick followed by stopAgent's `awaitMemoryFileFinalFlush` (session-manager.ts:2419), or a hot-reload that restarts the timer, can both fall in the same minute; memory-cue.ts:90-93 added a nanoid suffix for exactly this reason.
  Impact: The earlier summary of that minute is silently replaced; the scanner re-indexes the new content and the old delta is gone.
  Fix: Append seconds or a nanoid(4) suffix to the filename as writeCueMemory does.
- [low/test-gap] **No tests cover the failure paths that matter for recall**
  Evidence: src/memory/__tests__/memory-flush.test.ts:131-146 asserts the skip only with `getTurnsSince: () => []`; nothing tests that a skipped or empty-summary tick advances lastFlushAt, nor the 10s abort with a summarizer that returns "". memory-scanner.test.ts has no subagent-path case; turn-dispatcher.test.ts:576-622 exercises handleTaskToolReturn only by calling it directly; session-summarizer.test.ts has no case for repeated flushSessionMidway growth or for prompt quality on long sessions; compaction.test.ts never checks that `summary` is persisted.
  Impact: The high-severity findings above all pass the suite green.
  Fix: Add the five tests named here alongside the fixes.

**Strengths.**

- Atomic temp+rename writes shared by the disk flush, cue and subagent writers (memory-flush.ts:133-150) and a chokidar awaitWriteFinish window keep the scanner from indexing half-written files.
- summarizeSession never throws, is idempotent on status, archives raw turns to session_archive before pruning, and marks sessions summarized so crash drains cannot double-write (session-summarizer.ts:188-463).
- Restart hygiene is sound: a fresh ConversationStore session per start plus closeStaleActiveSessions marking orphans crashed (conversation-store.ts:223-233) feeds the bounded summarize-pending drain (5 per tick), so SIGKILL does not strand sessions.
- The daily-log flusher is cursor-gated and exactly-once across ticks and restarts (session-log-flusher.ts:103-124), decoupling consolidation input from compaction.
- sdk-transcript-reader is defensive and fail-open: validates every block at runtime, resolves tool names out of order, and falls back to ConversationStore text so compaction can never fail because of the transcript (sdk-transcript-reader.ts:239-310).
- Scanner idempotency via sha256 in memory_files avoids re-embedding unchanged files on boot backfill (memory-scanner.ts:208-214), and per-chunk inserts are transactional across the four tables (store.ts:2389-2452).
- Insert-time dedup merges tags, keeps max importance, and invalidates the v2 vector so the migration runner re-embeds (dedup.ts:84-131); summary dedupe is FK-safe, re-points sessions and archives, and runs one transaction per group (summary-dedupe.ts:215-282).
- Cue files are treated as standing memory that survives the 14-day window (memory-chunks.ts:197-207), matching the user's expectation for 'remember this'.
- Retrieval default excludeTags stops the giant summary blobs from leaking into prompts (memory-retrieval.ts:540-548), a real past pollution bug.

### Conversation store, conversation search and briefs: src/memory/conversation-store.ts, conversation-types.ts, conversation-search.ts, conversation-search.types.ts, conversation-brief.ts, conversation-brief.types.ts, src/manager/conversation-brief-cache.ts (plus one-hop reads of time-window.ts, session-summarizer.ts, store.ts DDL, memory-lookup-handler.ts, session-config.ts, session-manager.ts lifecycle hooks, discord/capture.ts)

**How it works.** Storage. MemoryStore creates three base tables in the per-agent memories.db: conversation_sessions and conversation_turns (src/memory/store.ts:1835-1878), an external-content FTS5 index conversation_turns_fts kept in sync by AI/AD/AU triggers with tokenizer `unicode61 remove_diacritics 2` (store.ts:1939-1964), and session_archive plus session_archive_fts with the same trigger pattern (store.ts:2011-2058). ConversationStore receives the raw better-sqlite3 handle (conversation-store.ts:190-193) and prepares every statement up front (conversation-store.ts:1152-1436).

Session lifecycle. SessionManager.startAgent calls startSession then closeStaleActiveSessions so a previous unclean run's row flips to crashed (src/manager/session-manager.ts:1298-1303). stopAgent calls endSession and then awaits summarizeSession (session-manager.ts:2425-2434); the crash handler calls crashSession and fires summarizeSession without awaiting (3899-3907). A heartbeat check drains anything left in ended/crashed with turns and no summary via listPendingSummarySessions (session-manager.ts:4110; SQL at conversation-store.ts:1220-1233). A daily session-retention check closes orphaned active rows older than 24h and deletes empty terminated rows older than 30 days (src/heartbeat/checks/session-retention.ts:64-69).

Turn capture. The only writer of live turns is captureDiscordExchange, which records a user turn (with instruction-detector flags) and an assistant turn per Discord exchange (src/discord/capture.ts:87-105); bridge.ts invokes it at three sites, all with isTrustedChannel:true (bridge.ts:2825, 6252, 8231). recordTurn assigns turn_index from the session's turn_count inside one transaction (conversation-store.ts:508-549). Historical OpenClaw sessions are inserted directly with INSERT OR IGNORE and is_trusted_channel=1 (src/sync/conversation-turn-translator.ts:269-273).

Relation to memories. summarizeSession loads the turns (src/memory/session-summarizer.ts:231), produces a Haiku summary, a short-session line, or a raw-turn dump, inserts a MemoryEntry tagged `session-summary` and `session:<id>` with skipDedup (315-335), calls markSummarized (356), writes the full role-prefixed transcript to session_archive via archiveSession (392; conversation-store.ts:983-1021), then deletes the raw turns (415; conversation-store.ts:600-603). So raw turns live only until summarization; afterwards a session is represented by its session row, one summary memory, and one archive row. A separate 15-minute flush writes cumulative `mid-session` memories while the session is active without touching turns (session-summarizer.ts:491-660).

Search (agent tool). The agent sees one tool, memory_lookup, with query/limit/scope/page/since/until (src/mcp/server.ts:1565-1617). It goes over IPC to invokeMemoryLookup (src/manager/daemon.ts:15956-16010; memory-lookup-handler.ts:128-261). scope=memories with page 0 and no window uses legacy GraphSearch; otherwise, or when that returns nothing and scope was omitted, it calls searchByScope (handler 170-234). searchByScope merges four lexical paths: substring over the 200 most recent memories (conversation-search.ts:114-142), substring over every `session-summary` memory (148-172), FTS over live turns (178-212), FTS over the archive (230-273); dedup runs only for scope=all (278-279); score is 0.7 relevance + 0.3 decay (360-374); pages are capped at 10 (83-86). Time windows are parsed by time-window.ts and pushed into SQL (conversation-store.ts:636-663, 874-895). The dashboard-only IPC cases search-conversations and list-recent-conversations (daemon.ts:9852-9947) are operator surfaces, not agent tools. Per-turn auto-injection deliberately excludes session-summary, mid-session and raw-fallback memories (src/memory/memory-retrieval.ts:259).

Brief (injected). At startAgent, buildSessionConfig computes a fingerprint over listRecentTerminatedSessions(sessionCount), consults ConversationBriefCache, else calls assembleConversationBrief (src/manager/session-config.ts:1117-1164). The brief skips when the last terminated session ended less than gapThresholdHours ago (conversation-brief.ts:150-170), otherwise takes every `session-summary` memory, sorts by memory createdAt, slices to sessionCount (176-180), accumulates whole summaries within budgetTokens (199-225), renders raw-fallback entries as a one-line placeholder (117-121), and the result lands in the mutable suffix as conversation_context (src/manager/context-assembler.ts:445, 1326, 1520). The cache is invalidated on stop and crash only (session-manager.ts:2371, 3920).

**Data flow.** Enters: Discord exchange -> captureDiscordExchange -> ConversationStore.recordTurn -> conversation_turns (+ conversation_turns_fts via trigger conversation_turns_ai). OpenClaw history -> conversation-turn-translator tryInsertTurn -> same tables. Sessions: startSession / endSession / crashSession / closeStaleActiveSessions / closeOrphanedActiveSessions -> conversation_sessions.

Transforms: summarizeSession (session-summarizer.ts) reads getTurnsForSession, writes memories (tags session-summary, session:<id>) via MemoryStore.insert, sets conversation_sessions.summary_memory_id via markSummarized, writes session_archive via archiveSession (+ session_archive_fts via trigger), then deleteTurnsForSession removes the raw turns (trigger conversation_turns_ad clears the FTS rows). flushSessionMidway reads the same turns every 15 min and writes mid-session memories. backfillSessionArchive (boot, one-shot) copies summary memory content into session_archive for pre-archive sessions.

Reads that reach the agent: (1) memory_lookup MCP tool -> invokeMemoryLookup -> searchByScope -> MemoryStore.listRecent/listInRange/findByTag + ConversationStore.searchTurns/listTurnsInRange/searchArchive/listArchiveInRange; results return as content snippets with origin and session_id. (2) assembleConversationBrief -> listRecentTerminatedSessions (gap) + MemoryStore.findByTag("session-summary") -> conversation_context section at session start. (3) bridge.ts fast lane reads getTurnsForSession(sessionId, 8) for the last 8 turns. (4) restart-greeting reads listRecentTerminatedSessions + getTurnsForSession. Other readers: session-log-flush (daily markdown log from turns), MemoryFlushTimer (disk flush from turns), dream getRecentSummaries (listRecentTerminatedSessions -> summary memory), memory-census getSessionHealth, F27 dashboard IPC (searchTurns, listRecentSessions).

Leaves: pruneEmptyTerminatedSessions deletes empty terminated session rows older than 30 days; deleteTurnsForSession deletes turns at summarization; nothing ever deletes session_archive rows, summary memories, or mid-session memories.

**Config knobs parsed but not wired at runtime.**

- `memory.conversation.enabled`: Intended to disable conversation persistence; never read. ConversationStore is always constructed (src/manager/session-memory.ts:168) and captureDiscordExchange always records.
- `memory.conversation.turnRetentionDays`: Intended age-based pruning of raw turns; never read anywhere (only src/memory/schema.ts:81 and the type at src/shared/types.ts:604). Turns are deleted only at summarization.

**Issues.**

- [high/never-retrieved] **listRecentTerminatedSessions hides every successfully summarized session because the summarizer deletes its turns**
  Evidence: conversation-store.ts:1203-1208 (the SQL): `AND status IN ('ended', 'crashed', 'summarized') AND EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM conversation_turns ct WHERE ct.session_id = conversation_sessions.id LIMIT 1 )`. session-summarizer.ts:415 runs `deps.conversationStore.deleteTurnsForSession(sessionId)` right after markSummarized succeeds, so a summarized session has zero turn rows and fails the EXISTS. Consumers that depend on the list: conversation-brief.ts:150 `const recent = conversationStore.listRecentTerminatedSessions(agentName, 1);` (gap check); session-config.ts:1127-1130 fingerprint; dream-machinery.ts:283-290 `conversationStore.listRecentTerminatedSessions(agent, limit)` then `if (!s.summaryMemoryId) return null;`; restart-greeting.ts:443-447 and the fallback loop at 473-476 that iterates the already-filtered `recent`. The unit test conversation-store.test.ts:177-193 seeds a turn and never prunes, which is why it passes.
  Impact: The 4-hour gap skip never fires once a session has been summarized (the knob resumeGapThresholdHours is inert in practice). The dream pass's getRecentSummaries returns nothing for summarized sessions (summary memories are never fed to dream). The restart greeting's summaryMemoryId fallback (restart-greeting.ts:464-485) is dead code and the greeting degrades to 'no prior session to recap' after every clean stop. The brief cache fingerprint is computed over the wrong set.
  Fix: Change the predicate to `AND (summary_memory_id IS NOT NULL OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM conversation_turns ...) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM session_archive a WHERE a.session_id = conversation_sessions.id))` and add a test that runs summarizeSession (with prune) and then asserts the session is still listed.
- [high/design-gap] **Conversation FTS queries are phrase-quoted as a whole, so multi-word natural-language queries almost never match**
  Evidence: conversation-store.ts:46-51: `const escaped = trimmed.replace(/"/g, '""'); return `"${escaped}"`;` used by searchTurns (line 626 `const escaped = escapeFtsQuery(query);`) and searchArchive (line 862). FTS5 treats a quoted string as an adjacent-token phrase. The index tokenizer has no stemming: store.ts:1942 `tokenize='unicode61 remove_diacritics 2'` (and 2036 for the archive). The repo already fixed this exact problem for memory chunks: src/memory/fts-query.ts:5-10 'The raw message under FTS5's implicit AND matched nothing ... Multi-word questions therefore matched nothing' and fts-query.ts:57-61 buildFtsMatchQuery ORs quoted terms. Operator directive config/schema.ts:267 tells agents to 'extract 2-4 noun phrases as search terms'.
  Impact: A query like 'deploy cadence staging' only matches a turn or transcript containing exactly that phrase; the raw-turn and archive paths contribute nearly nothing for the queries the system instructs agents to write, so cross-session recall through memory_lookup scope=conversations is far weaker than the design assumes.
  Fix: In searchTurns/searchArchive replace escapeFtsQuery with `buildFtsMatchQuery(query) ?? '""'` from fts-query.ts (OR of quoted terms; bm25 still ranks multi-term matches higher). Optionally rebuild both FTS tables with `porter unicode61` in a guarded migration.
- [high/design-gap] **searchByScope memory and session-summary paths are whole-string substring matches; the injected embedder is never used**
  Evidence: conversation-search.ts:312-315: `function contentMatches(content, normalizedQuery) { if (normalizedQuery.length === 0) return false; return content.toLowerCase().includes(normalizedQuery); }` applied at 121 and 152. Candidate pool: 115-117 `deps.memoryStore.listRecent(MEMORY_CANDIDATE_POOL)` with `MEMORY_CANDIDATE_POOL = 200` (66). conversation-search.types.ts:77-81 declares `readonly embedder: EmbeddingService;` in ScopedSearchDeps, but `deps.embedder` is not referenced anywhere in conversation-search.ts. memory-lookup-handler.ts:180-200 routes the implicit-scope fallback (legacy search returned nothing) into this path with scope 'all'.
  Impact: For scope=conversations/all (and for the Gap-4 fallback that fires exactly when semantic search found nothing), memories and session summaries only match if the entire query string appears verbatim; memories older than the 200 most recent are never considered. The agent gets 'nothing found' for most real questions about past sessions.
  Fix: Smallest change: match on extractFtsTerms(query) with all (or most) terms present instead of the whole string, for both paths. Better: for the memory path reuse GraphSearch/semantic search with the already-injected embedder, and query summaries through memories_fts with buildFtsMatchQuery.
- [medium/design-gap] **Archive hits return the first 500 characters of the transcript, not the matching passage**
  Evidence: conversation-search.ts:259-260 builds the archive result with `content: a.fullText`; 330-333 `function makeSnippet(content) { if (content.length <= SNIPPET_MAX_CHARS) return content; return content.slice(0, SNIPPET_MAX_CHARS) + '…'; }`; memory-lookup-handler.ts:239 returns `content: r.snippet, // orchestrator truncates to SNIPPET_MAX_CHARS`. session_archive.full_text is the whole session (conversation-store.ts:990 `turns.map((t) => `${t.role}: ${t.content}`).join('\n')`).
  Impact: When the archive is the only surviving source for a consolidated session, the agent sees 'user: hi ...' from the start of a long session instead of the lines that matched its query, so the hit is usually useless even when ranking is right.
  Fix: Project `snippet(session_archive_fts, 0, '', '', '…', 48) AS snippet` in the four archive statements and carry it through buildResult as the snippet for origin session-archive (keep fullText in content).
- [medium/bug] **scope='conversations' never deduplicates, so each summarized session appears twice (summary + archive)**
  Evidence: conversation-search.ts:278-279: `const deduped = options.scope === 'all' ? dedupPreferSummary(candidates) : candidates;`. Every summarized session has both a session-summary memory (session-summarizer.ts:315-335) and an archive row (392) that match the same terms. The only collision test runs with scope 'all' (conversation-search-archive-bucket.test.ts:203-216).
  Impact: Up to half of each 10-result page in the scope the tool description recommends for 'older Discord conversation history' is duplicate content from the same session, pushing distinct sessions off the page.
  Fix: Apply dedupPreferSummary whenever scope !== 'memories' (it only drops turn/archive rows that have a summary sibling, so conversations-scope semantics are preserved).
- [medium/race] **Concurrent summarizers (stop vs heartbeat drain, crash vs stop) write duplicate session-summary memories**
  Evidence: conversation-store.ts:372-379 documents it: 'the crash-path fire-and-forget summarizer and the stop-path awaited summarizer both run to completion (each observes status=ended/crashed in its own snapshot...)' and markSummarized now returns silently for the loser. session-summarizer.ts:323-333 inserts with `skipDedup: true` before any claim; session-manager.ts:2432 (stop, awaited), 3906 (crash, detached) and 4110 (drain via listPendingSummarySessions, which includes a session the stop path is summarizing right now because status is already 'ended'). conversation-brief.ts:176-180 renders every session-summary memory with no per-session dedup.
  Impact: A session can produce two or more summary memories (two Haiku calls, two embeddings); the brief then spends one of its three slots on a duplicate, and search returns both.
  Fix: Claim before summarizing: `UPDATE conversation_sessions SET summary_memory_id = 'pending' WHERE id = ? AND status IN ('ended','crashed') AND summary_memory_id IS NULL`; skip if changes = 0; replace with the real id in markSummarized. Alternatively dedupe brief and search candidates by the `session:<id>` tag, keeping the earliest.
- [medium/design-gap] **Brief and search date session summaries by memory createdAt, so backlog-drained old sessions render as 'Session from today (just now)' and displace recent ones**
  Evidence: conversation-brief.ts:179 `.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt))` and 289-290 `const date = renderAgentVisibleTimestamp(mem.createdAt, agentTz).slice(0, 10); const when = formatRelativeTime(mem.createdAt, now);`. conversation-search.ts:151 filters summaries by `inWindow(m.createdAt, window)`. The summary insert passes no session timestamp (session-summarizer.ts:323-335) and the drain processes oldest first (conversation-store.ts:1232 `ORDER BY started_at ASC`). MemoryStore.insert has no occurredAt field (grep of store.ts finds none).
  Impact: After the heartbeat drains a historical backlog, the brief can show summaries of weeks-old sessions labelled with today's date while the real last session falls out of the top 3; time-window browsing of summaries uses summarization time rather than conversation time.
  Fix: When rendering/sorting, resolve the session id from the `session:` tag and use conversationStore.getSession(id).endedAt (point lookup) for the heading, relative time, sort key and window filter; fall back to createdAt when the session row is gone.
- [medium/inert-config] **`enabled` and `turnRetentionDays` are parsed but never read; turns of never-summarized sessions persist forever and are retried every heartbeat**
  Evidence: schema.ts:80-81 `enabled: z.boolean().default(true), turnRetentionDays: z.number().int().min(7).default(90),`; repo-wide grep finds no runtime read (only src/shared/types.ts:604). session-memory.ts:168 `const conversationStore = new ConversationStore(store.getDatabase());` unconditionally; capture.ts:87 records unconditionally. Retention is only the summarization prune (session-summarizer.ts:415) and the empty-row delete (session-retention.ts:69). A session whose embed or insert fails returns skipped without writing (session-summarizer.ts:296-311, 337-351) and stays in listPendingSummarySessions (conversation-store.ts:1220-1233) with no attempt counter or backoff.
  Impact: Operators cannot disable capture or bound raw-turn age as the schema promises. Sessions that persistently fail summarization keep their raw turns indefinitely and cost a Haiku call per heartbeat tick for each.
  Fix: Either remove the two knobs from the schema, or wire them: gate startSession/recordTurn on `enabled`, and add to session-retention.ts a daily `DELETE FROM conversation_turns WHERE created_at < ?` (cutoff = now - turnRetentionDays) restricted to sessions that are terminated. Add a bounded retry (e.g. skip sessions with ended_at within the last 10 min, and stop after N attempts recorded in a column).
- [medium/design-gap] **After an unclean restart the previous session is summarized minutes later by the drain, but the brief was already built and is not refreshed until the next stop**
  Evidence: session-manager.ts:1298-1303 starts the new session and `closeStaleActiveSessions(name, convSession.id)` (closes the old one as crashed) and then calls `buildSessionConfig` at 1320, which reads `memoryStore.findByTag('session-summary')` (conversation-brief.ts:176). The crashed row is only summarized by the heartbeat drain (session-manager.ts:4095-4110). The brief cache is invalidated only at 2371 (stop) and 3920 (crash), never after the drain.
  Impact: After a daemon kill, OOM or power loss, the agent's whole next run lacks the summary of what it was last doing; cross-session continuity depends on a clean stop.
  Fix: In startAgent, after closeStaleActiveSessions and before buildSessionConfig, `await` summarizePendingSessions(name, 1) under a bounded timeout (or synchronously archive + short-summarize the just-closed row), so the brief includes it.
- [medium/performance] **Mid-session flush memories are cumulative, never removed after the final summary, and surface in memory_lookup as ordinary memories**
  Evidence: session-summarizer.ts:524 `const turns = deps.conversationStore.getTurnsForSession(sessionId);` (all turns, every flush), 601-605 tags `['mid-session', `session:${sessionId}`, `flush:${flushSequence}`]`, 613-623 insert with `skipDedup: true`; default interval 15 min (session-manager.ts:3977). Repo-wide grep for 'mid-session' shows no deletion or demotion anywhere. conversation-search.ts:119-121 skips only `tags.includes('session-summary')`, so these rows enter the memory path as origin 'memory' and are not covered by dedupPreferSummary (401-405).
  Impact: A continuously active agent accumulates roughly 96 overlapping summaries per day that are never reconciled with the final session summary; they inflate the memories table without bound and compete with real memories in search results.
  Fix: On summarizeSession success, delete (or demote to cold) memories tagged both `mid-session` and `session:<id>`; and in searchByScope treat `mid-session` rows as session-derived so dedupPreferSummary drops them when a summary exists.
- [medium/design-gap] **Only Discord exchanges are recorded as turns; scheduled, HTTP/OpenAI, CLI and subagent turns leave no conversation record**
  Evidence: Repo-wide grep for `.recordTurn(` on a ConversationStore finds a single caller, src/discord/capture.ts:87 and :99, invoked from bridge.ts:2817, 6239, 8223. The translator (conversation-turn-translator.ts:269) only imports historical OpenClaw files. Yet a session row is opened for every agent at start (session-manager.ts:1298) and the hygiene comment (session-retention.ts:5-9) records 'hundreds of active rows per agent, most of them with zero turns'.
  Impact: Non-Discord work produces no turns, so no summary, no archive row and nothing searchable; the agent cannot recall what it did in scheduled or API-driven sessions, and empty session rows are generated for them.
  Fix: Call the same capture helper (channelId null, isTrustedChannel true for operator-originated input) from the non-Discord turn dispatch path, or skip startSession for agents that have no turn source.
- [low/design-gap] **One untrusted turn marks the whole session archive untrusted and removes the entire transcript from search**
  Evidence: conversation-store.ts:991 `const hasUntrustedTurn = turns.some((t) => t.isTrustedChannel === false);` and the default filter at 1402 `AND s.has_untrusted_turn = 0`. In practice the gate is constant: every live capture passes `isTrustedChannel: true` (bridge.ts:2825, 6252, 8231) and the translator writes 1 (conversation-turn-translator.ts:273), so no untrusted rows exist today.
  Impact: If an untrusted channel is ever captured, a single message hides hundreds of trusted turns from retrieval; today the SEC-01 trust filter provides no actual separation because everything is trusted.
  Fix: Build full_text from trusted turns only (so has_untrusted_turn stays 0 and the session remains searchable), or store per-turn trust in the archive text and filter at snippet time.
- [low/design-gap] **No agent scoping in the brief or search when a derived session shares the parent's database**
  Evidence: conversation-brief.ts:176 `const all = memoryStore.findByTag('session-summary');` (no agent filter); conversation-search.ts:149 and 180-190 pass no agent to findByTag/searchTurns; conversation_turns has no agent column (store.ts:1854-1868). session-retention.ts:11 'derived sessions share the parent's DB'; subagent-config-overrides.ts:155 notes mid-session entries written 'into the shared' store.
  Impact: A parent's brief and search results can include a subagent's session summaries and turns, and vice versa; provenance of which agent held the conversation is lost once the summary is a plain memory.
  Fix: Add an `agent:<name>` tag to summary and flush memories and filter on it in the brief; have searchTurns/listTurnsInRange join conversation_sessions and filter by agent_name when a name is supplied.
- [low/bug] **archiveSession copies the session's turn_count/total_tokens counters, which are known to be wrong for translator-imported sessions**
  Evidence: conversation-store.ts:999-1000 passes `session.turnCount, session.totalTokens` into the archive row although `turns` was just loaded at 989. The store itself says at 1193-1195 'turn_count field is unreliable post-cutover-translation'; the translator inserts the session with `0, 0` (conversation-turn-translator.ts:225) and never increments.
  Impact: session_archive.turn_count is 0 for every imported session and the 'archived session before prune' log (session-summarizer.ts:393-400) under-reports.
  Fix: Use `turns.length` and the sum of `tokenCount` when writing the archive row.
- [low/design-gap] **FTS over-fetch is fixed at 30 from offset 0 for every page**
  Evidence: conversation-search.ts:183 and 187 `limit: MAX_RESULTS_PER_PAGE * FTS5_OVERFETCH_MULTIPLIER, offset: 0,` (same at 235-241 for the archive); the page is then sliced from the merged list at 295 and hasMore computed from that pool at 296.
  Impact: Turn/archive candidates past the 30th FTS hit can never be paged to; hasMore reports false even when FTS has more matches.
  Fix: Over-fetch `offset + clampedLimit * FTS5_OVERFETCH_MULTIPLIER` so later pages can reach deeper FTS results.
- [low/silent-failure] **Embedding/insert failures in summarizeSession are reported with the misleading reason 'session-not-terminal'**
  Evidence: session-summarizer.ts:307-311 and 346-350 both return `reason: 'session-not-terminal' as const` after logging 'embedding failed' / 'memoryStore.insert failed'; session-manager.ts:4197-4204 logs that reason as the skip cause.
  Impact: Operators reading 'session summarization skipped reason=session-not-terminal' cannot tell an embedder outage from a lifecycle bug, and the drain's skipped count hides persistent failures.
  Fix: Add distinct reasons ('embed-failed', 'insert-failed') to SummarizeSessionResult.
- [low/test-gap] **No test covers the summarize-then-prune interplay with listRecentTerminatedSessions, the brief, or conversations-scope duplication**
  Evidence: conversation-store.test.ts:177-193 asserts summarized sessions are listed but seeds a turn and never deletes it. conversation-store-archive-e2e.test.ts:80-123 runs the archive path but never asserts on listRecentTerminatedSessions or assembleConversationBrief afterwards. The only summary-vs-archive collision test uses scope 'all' (conversation-search-archive-bucket.test.ts:203-216). listTurnsInRange/listArchiveInRange have no direct store-level tests (only via the time-window searchByScope tests at conversation-search.test.ts:554-643).
  Impact: The highest-impact regression in this subsystem (issue 1) is invisible to the suite.
  Fix: Add an e2e test: record turns, summarizeSession with prune, then assert listRecentTerminatedSessions includes the session, the brief gap check sees it, and a scope='conversations' search returns one result per session.

**Strengths.**

- FTS5 external-content indexes with AI/AD/AU triggers keep both turn and archive indexes consistent with zero application code, and the archive upsert correctly uses ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE to preserve rowids (conversation-store.ts:1326-1352).
- The session state machine is enforced in SQL (status predicates on each UPDATE) and markSummarized is idempotent, so the documented stop/crash double-run cannot corrupt state (conversation-store.ts:383-411).
- recordTurn is transactional and the UNIQUE (session_id, turn_index, role) index gives the translator a clean idempotent import path (conversation-store.ts:508-549; store.ts:1869-1870).
- The durable session_archive design is sound: raw text survives the prune, the one-shot backfill is transactional and guarded by a migrations row, and summary-only rows are clearly provenance-tagged (conversation-store.ts:1060-1149).
- Query escaping never throws on agent-written text, and the SEC-01 trust filter is applied consistently to results and counts in all four paths, including the time-bounded variants (conversation-store.ts:626-710, 862-956).
- Time windows are pushed into SQL rather than post-filtered, so old windows are not starved by newer high-ranking matches, and the archive overlap predicate correctly treats a missing ended_at (conversation-store.ts:874-895).
- assembleConversationBrief is pure, budget-accumulates whole summaries rather than truncating mid-summary, downgrades raw-fallback dumps to a one-line placeholder, and escalates to error logging when context loss is likely (conversation-brief.ts:199-258).
- Hygiene methods (closeStaleActiveSessions, closeOrphanedActiveSessions, pruneEmptyTerminatedSessions) are narrow, fork-guarded in the heartbeat, and never touch rows that still carry turns or an archive (conversation-store.ts:223-276; session-retention.ts:39-69).
- Everything returned is frozen and the modules are dependency-injected, which is why the test suite can exercise decay, pagination and SEC-01 behavior deterministically.

### Consolidation and dreaming: weekly/monthly digests (consolidation.ts), the per-agent dream pass (prompt builder, LLM call, JSON parse), the D-10 auto-apply and veto window with promote-to-MEMORY.md, graph edges, dream and consolidation run logs, the weekly memory digest, and the cron, IPC and CLI wiring in daemon.ts

**How it works.** All paths are relative to /Users/jasjagpal/Development/projects/clawcode-platform/.

Consolidation. At daemon boot a per-agent TaskScheduler entry named memory-consolidation (default cron 0 3 * * *) is registered (src/manager/daemon.ts:4669-4745); it resolves the MemoryStore at fire time. runConsolidation (src/memory/consolidation.ts:566-749) scans <memoryPath>/memory for YYYY-MM-DD.md daily logs (produced by the heartbeat session-log flusher, src/manager/session-log-flusher.ts:1-27), groups them by ISO week, skips the in-progress week (:133-137, :166) and requires weeklyThreshold files (fleet default 4 at src/config/schema.ts:3675; daemon fallback 7 at daemon.ts:4677). Each group becomes a 30K-char prompt (:262-307), is summarized by summarizeWithHaiku (daemon.ts:4716), guarded by isErrorSummary (src/memory/error-guard.ts:49-57), then writeWeeklyDigest writes digests/weekly-YYYY-Www.md and inserts a memories row with source=consolidation, importance 0.7, tags [weekly-digest, week] (:354-405). Only after that are the dailies renamed into archive/YYYY/ and their session_logs rows deleted (:471-509). Monthly digests repeat the pattern over weekly files (:194-251, :411-458) and archive the weeklies. Every run appends started and completed/failed rows to ~/.clawcode/manager/consolidation-runs.jsonl (src/manager/consolidation-run-log.ts:67-84).

Dreaming. The daemon builds one DreamMachinery (daemon.ts:3987-4030) and registers one croner job per resolved agent with pattern */min(idleMinutes,59) (src/manager/dream-cron.ts:211-215; daemon.ts:13321-13362); agents with dream.enabled false get no job (:196-197). Each tick: shouldFirePriorityPass counts tier-1 truncation events, 2 in 24h (src/manager/dream-cron.ts:66-89), which are recorded whenever MEMORY.md was cut at injection (src/manager/session-config.ts:751-757); isAgentIdle compares now against the newest conversation turn across the last 5 sessions (src/manager/dream-machinery.ts:179-213; src/manager/idle-window-detector.ts:67-82: 5-minute floor, 6-hour ceiling). When idle, runDreamPass (src/manager/dream-pass.ts:216-353) reads the 30 newest memory_chunks by file mtime (src/memory/store.ts:2494-2505), ${tier1Dir}/MEMORY.md, the summaries of the last 3 terminated sessions, and ${tier1Dir}/graph-edges.json, builds a prompt capped at 32K tokens with graded degradation (src/manager/dream-prompt-builder.ts:263-351), calls callHaikuDirect (daemon.ts:4004-4010), extracts a brace-balanced JSON object and zod-validates it into newWikilinks, promotionCandidates, themedReflection and suggestedConsolidations.

Apply. applyDreamResult (dream-machinery.ts:433-516) resolves the policy (autoPromote true, floor 80, window 30 min; :60-68) and runs applyDreamResultD10 (src/manager/dream-auto-apply.ts:376-559). Row 1 appends deduped edges to graph-edges.json (src/manager/dream-graph-edges.ts:50-99). A dream-log section is appended to ${tier1Dir}/dreams/YYYY-MM-DD.md (src/manager/dream-log-writer.ts:157-189). Candidates fork: priority pass makes all eligible; edit/merge/overwrite are operator-only; additive candidates with score >= floor are eligible (:457-476). Eligible candidates, with a snapshot of the chunk text, are appended as a pending row to ~/.clawcode/manager/dream-veto-pending.jsonl with deadline now+window (:482-509; src/manager/dream-veto-store.ts:229-240) and a summary with veto/approve commands is posted to the agent's channel (src/manager/dream-discord-summary.ts:58-120). One row per attempt lands in dream-runs.jsonl (src/manager/dream-run-log.ts:94-113).

Veto loop. registerDreamVetoTick runs every minute (src/manager/dream-veto-tick.ts:49-84; daemon.ts:13395-13403). tick reduces the JSONL to latest-per-runId and for pending rows past deadline calls applyDreamVetoRow (daemon.ts:3941-3985), which runs promoteCandidates (src/manager/dream-promote.ts:94-149): use the live chunk or the snapshot, skip if the body is already a substring of MEMORY.md, skip if MEMORY.md would exceed INJECTED_MEMORY_MAX_CHARS (16,000), else append '## heading / body / _Promoted by the dream pass..._' through the jailed clawcodeMemoryEdit append. The veto IPC appends a vetoed row; approve sets deadline 0 and runs the tick at once (daemon.ts:10780-10812). MEMORY.md is what context-assembler injects (src/manager/context-assembler.ts:769), so promotion is the only dream output that reaches a prompt.

Manual path. clawcode dream <agent> (src/cli/commands/dream.ts:103-181) calls run-dream-pass; handleRunDreamPassIpc (daemon.ts:1505-1558) honours force and idleBypass and uses the same machinery.

Weekly digest. scheduleMemoryDigestCron (src/manager/memory-digest-cron.ts:141-149; daemon.ts:13124-13137) posts on Mondays 09:00 PT up to 8 of the week's memories rows, importance-sorted and excluding session-summary/mid-session/raw-fallback/confirmed (src/manager/memory-digest.ts:73-101), each with an 8-char id code. A number reaction deletes that memory; a thumbs-up sets importance 0.9 and tags it confirmed (:167-199).

**Data flow.** Entry points: (1) conversation turns become <memoryPath>/memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md via flushNewTurnsToSessionLog (heartbeat check src/heartbeat/checks/session-log-flush.ts); the memory scanner indexes every memory/**/*.md into memory_chunks (src/memory/memory-scanner.ts:44-57). (2) runConsolidation reads those dailies (consolidation.ts:638-641), writes memory/digests/weekly-*.md and monthly-*.md plus memories rows (writeWeeklyDigest/writeMonthlyDigest), moves dailies to memory/archive/YYYY/ and weeklies to memory/archive/digests/ (archiveDailyLogs/archiveWeeklyDigests), deletes session_logs rows, and appends to consolidation-runs.jsonl. Digest .md files sit inside memoryDir so the scanner also indexes them as chunks; both the chunk and the memories row are reachable by hybrid retrieval (src/memory/memory-retrieval.ts) and the memories row by the weekly digest post. (3) runDreamPass reads memory_chunks (store.listRecentMemoryChunks), ${tier1Dir}/MEMORY.md, terminated-session summaries via conversationStore.listRecentTerminatedSessions + memoryStore.getById(summaryMemoryId), and ${tier1Dir}/graph-edges.json; it writes nothing. applyDreamResultD10 writes graph-edges.json (appendDreamWikilinks), ${tier1Dir}/dreams/YYYY-MM-DD.md (writeDreamLog), dream-veto-pending.jsonl (vetoStore.scheduleAutoApply), a Discord post (postToAgent) and dream-runs.jsonl (appendDreamRun). (4) The veto tick reads dream-veto-pending.jsonl (readAllRows), and promoteCandidates reads roots.memoryMdPath and appends to ${tier1Dir}/MEMORY.md through clawcodeMemoryEdit; MEMORY.md is then re-read by the prompt loader on the next session build (context-assembler) and re-indexed only if nested under memory/. graph-edges.json is read only by the next dream pass. dreams/*.md is read by the dashboard (daemon.ts:10595, 10680, 10851) and the /clawcode-status panel (slash-commands.ts:2346) and never by a prompt builder, except indirectly through the scanner when memoryPath is <workspace>/memory. (5) The memory digest reads memories via store.listInRange, posts to Discord, and reaction feedback calls store.delete or an UPDATE on memories (applyDigestFeedback); it never touches MEMORY.md, memory_chunks or any file. Exits from the system: archived files are never deleted; graph-edges.json, dreams/, dream-runs.jsonl, dream-veto-pending.jsonl and consolidation-runs.jsonl are append-only with no retention.

**Config knobs parsed but not wired at runtime.**

- `dream.model`: Threaded into dispatchReq.model (dream-pass.ts:253-258) and reported in outcome.model and the dream log, but the daemon dispatch wrapper never forwards it and callHaikuDirect is pinned to haiku (daemon.ts:4004-4010; haiku-direct.ts:290). No runtime effect.
- `dream.retentionDays`: Only echoed to the dashboard (daemon.ts:10763). No code prunes dreams/*.md or any dream artifact.
- `memory.consolidation.summaryModel`: Parsed at src/memory/schema.ts:25 and typed at src/shared/types.ts:572; the daemon never reads it and always calls summarizeWithHaiku (daemon.ts:4716, 15900).
- `clawcode dream --priority`: Sent as params.priority (cli/commands/dream.ts:122) but RunDreamPassRequest has no priority field and the handler never reads it (daemon.ts:1456-1461, 7756-7770); applyDreamResult is called without isPriorityPass (daemon.ts:7831-7832).
- `clawcode dream --model`: Becomes modelOverride and reaches runDreamPass(agent, model), then is dropped by the dispatch wrapper (see dream.model).

**Issues.**

- [high/design-gap] **An idle agent re-dreams on every cron tick; nothing records that it already dreamt**
  Evidence: dream-cron.ts:243-255 is the only gate: 'const idle = deps.isAgentIdle({ lastTurnAt: deps.getLastTurnAt(), idleMinutes: effectiveIdleMinutes, now: deps.now }); if (!idle.idle) { ... return; } try { const outcome = await deps.runDreamPass(deps.agentName);'. idle-window-detector.ts:75-80 returns idle:true whenever elapsed exceeds the threshold ('if (elapsedMs > deps.idleMinutes * 60_000) { return { idle: true, reason: "idle-threshold-met" }; }') and the 6-hour ceiling also returns idle:true even though the header (:20-21) says 'STILL fire one final consolidation pass'. dream-machinery.ts:187-200 derives lastTurnAt only from conversation turns; grep for lastDreamAt finds it only in status-render.ts (display). The cron pattern is '*/${stepMinutes} * * * *' (dream-cron.ts:215).
  Impact: With idleMinutes 30, an agent that goes quiet for a weekend runs 48 dream passes per day on near-identical input (about 32K tokens each), appends 48 dream-log sections, schedules 48 veto rows for the same candidates, and posts a '[dream-pass] Promotion candidates' message to its channel every 30 minutes whenever Haiku names any candidate. Once MEMORY.md is near the cap this is pure cost plus channel noise.
  Fix: Keep a per-agent lastDreamAt in the machinery (or read the newest dream-runs.jsonl row for the agent) and skip the tick when lastDreamAt > lastTurnAt and no memory_chunks row has file_mtime_ms newer than lastDreamAt. Treat the ceiling as one-shot by the same rule.
- [high/design-gap] **Priority pass is self-defeating: triggered by MEMORY.md being over cap, but promotion refuses to write when MEMORY.md is at cap, and nothing compacts**
  Evidence: Trigger: session-config.ts:755-757 'if (tc && typeof tc.recordTier1TruncationEvent === "function") { try { tc.recordTier1TruncationEvent(config.name);' fires when MEMORY.md is truncated at injection; dream-cron.ts:82-86 fires priority at 2 events in 24h. Row 5: dream-auto-apply.ts:458-462 'if (isPriorityPass) { eligible.push(c); continue; }'. Apply: dream-promote.ts:118-125 'if (memory.length + section.length > deps.capChars) { skipped.push({ ... reason: "memory-at-cap" ... }); continue; }' and the module header :15-18 admits 'promoting into an over-cap file only feeds the truncation that triggers priority passes in the first place.' Mutating candidates are appended, not applied: :138 'downgraded: c.action === "edit" || c.action === "merge" || c.targetMode === "overwrite"'. suggestedConsolidations have no apply path anywhere (only rendered in dream-log-writer.ts:106-117 and dream-discord-summary.ts:96-99). The channel header promises 'Tier 1 over cap ,  proposed compaction' (dream-discord-summary.ts:60).
  Impact: Once MEMORY.md reaches 16,000 chars every scheduled promotion expires with memory-at-cap, the agent keeps dreaming on the shortened 5-minute threshold, the channel keeps announcing a compaction that never happens, and MEMORY.md never shrinks; the long-term memory channel is permanently closed for that agent.
  Fix: In applyDreamVetoRow, when readMemory().length + section.length > capChars on a priority row, archive the oldest '_Promoted by the dream pass_' sections through the existing clawcode_memory_archive primitive before appending; and in applyDreamResultD10 skip scheduling (surface only) when the current MEMORY.md length is already >= capChars.
- [high/design-gap] **Promotion appends verbatim with no supersession or contradiction check, and the only forget path cannot reach MEMORY.md**
  Evidence: dream-promote.ts:112-115 is the only duplicate check: 'if (memory.includes(body)) { skipped.push({ ... reason: "already-present" }); continue; }', then :127 'const res = await deps.appendMemory(section);'. Edits and merges are downgraded to appends (:138). The weekly digest's forget path deletes only a memories row: memory-digest.ts:195 'const deleted = store.delete(id);' while its header (:4-6) claims dreams are covered; applyDreamResultD10 never inserts into memories (no insert call in dream-auto-apply.ts or dream-promote.ts).
  Impact: A chunk saying 'X is Y' and a later chunk saying 'X is now Z' both land in MEMORY.md and are injected into every prompt side by side; a wrong promoted fact cannot be disputed through the digest and is never aged out, so MEMORY.md accretes contradictions until it hits the cap (which then triggers the issue above).
  Fix: At promote time run the existing hybrid retrieval over MEMORY.md sections for the candidate body and, above a similarity threshold, replace the older section instead of appending (or at least tag it superseded); also insert each promoted section as a memories row (source 'system', tag 'dream-promoted') so the weekly digest can surface it and a dispute can remove the MEMORY.md section by provenance line.
- [high/never-retrieved] **graph-edges.json wikilinks are a closed loop: written by the dream, read only by the next dream, never validated, never pruned**
  Evidence: dream-graph-edges.ts:7-9: 'The dream-pass primitive reads this file back in on the next pass to surface "existing wikilinks" context to the LLM'. A repo-wide grep for 'graph-edges' matches only dream-prompt-builder.ts, dream-pass.ts, dream-graph-edges.ts and dream-machinery.ts. The scanner indexes only .md (memory-scanner.ts:45 'if (!absPath.endsWith(".md")) return false;'). Edges are LLM strings persisted unchecked: dream-auto-apply.ts:407-411 'const linkArgs = outcome.result.newWikilinks.map((w) => ({ from: w.from, to: w.to }));' and dream-graph-edges.ts:94-97 'edges: [...existing, ...additions]' with no cap.
  Impact: Row 1, the only output the policy always auto-applies, changes nothing the agent ever sees; wikilinks_added in the run log and status panel is a vanity metric, and the file grows with edges to paths Haiku invented.
  Fix: Validate from/to exist under memoryDir before persisting, and feed surviving edges into memory_links (the store already has centrality-based hot promotion via tierConfig.centralityPromoteThreshold) so they influence retrieval; otherwise drop Row 1.
- [medium/inert-config] **dream.model and --model are silently ignored; every dream runs on Haiku but is logged under the configured model**
  Evidence: daemon.ts:4004-4010: 'const text = await callHaikuDirect(dispatchReq.systemPrompt, dispatchReq.userPrompt, { maxTokens: dispatchReq.maxOutputTokens }, "dream-pass");' (dispatchReq.model unused). haiku-direct.ts:290: 'return callModelDirect(systemPrompt, userPrompt, "haiku", opts, site, routeOverride);'. dream-pass.ts:343 reports 'model: deps.resolvedDreamConfig.model' and dream-log-writer.ts:74 prints it in the section header.
  Impact: An operator who sets dream.model: sonnet or runs clawcode dream --model opus gets Haiku output while the dream log, run log and CLI JSON claim sonnet/opus; reflection quality is lower than configured and provenance is wrong.
  Fix: In the daemon dispatch wrapper call callModelDirect(systemPrompt, userPrompt, dispatchReq.model, { maxTokens }, 'dream-pass').
- [medium/data-loss] **A digest file written before the SQLite insert blocks the week forever if the insert fails**
  Evidence: consolidation.ts:380-392: 'writeFileSync(filePath, markdown, "utf-8"); const embedding = await deps.embedder.embed(llmContent); deps.memoryStore.insert(' . Detection skips any week whose file exists: :169-170 'const digestFile = join(digestDir, `weekly-${key}.md`); if (existsSync(digestFile)) continue;'. The catch at :663-666 only records the error.
  Impact: If embed() or insert() throws (store closed by a concurrent restart, embedder error), the markdown digest exists, the memories row never does, the dailies are never archived, and no later run retries; the digest is unsearchable for good. The same ordering applies to monthly digests (:435-447).
  Fix: Embed and insert first, then write the file; or unlink the file in a catch around the insert.
- [medium/bug] **Dream artifacts are written under tier1Dir but read under memoryPath; MEMORY.md is read by name rather than by resolved path**
  Evidence: Writer root: daemon.ts:3996-3999 'return cfg ? resolveMemoryRoots(cfg).tier1Dir : "";' and dream-log-writer.ts:164 'const dir = `${memoryRoot}/dreams`;'. Readers: daemon.ts:10595, 10680, 10851 'const dreamsDir = joinPath(cfg.memoryPath, "dreams");' and slash-commands.ts:2346 'const dreamsDir = join(memBase, "dreams");'. memory-roots.ts:62-63: 'const tier1Dir = autoLoad ? dirname(resolve(autoLoad)) : stateRoot; const memoryMdPath = autoLoad ? resolve(autoLoad) : join(tier1Dir, "MEMORY.md");' with memoryAutoLoadPath unconstrained (schema.ts:2561). dream-pass.ts:235 reads '`${deps.memoryRoot}/MEMORY.md`' and applyDreamVetoRow reads roots.memoryMdPath but appends to path 'MEMORY.md' (daemon.ts:3958-3970).
  Impact: For an agent with memoryAutoLoadPath outside memoryPath (the fin-client hand-patch named in memory-roots.ts:11-13) the status panel says 'Dreamt: never' and the dashboard dream list is empty while dreams run; if the autoload file is not named MEMORY.md the dream pass reflects on a file the prompt does not load and promotion appends to a file the prompt does not inject.
  Fix: Use resolveMemoryRoots(cfg).tier1Dir in the three daemon readers and slash-commands, and pass roots.memoryMdPath into the dream pass and the promote appendMemory callback.
- [medium/never-injected] **themedReflection and consolidation suggestions never reach a prompt, except by accident through the scanner, where they pollute the next dream's input**
  Evidence: The scanner watches <workspace>/memory plus roots.memoryDir (daemon.ts:5470-5471 'return isWithinDir(join(workspacePath, "memory"), roots.memoryDir) ? [] : [roots.memoryDir];', memory-roots.ts:71 'const memoryDir = join(stateRoot, "memory");'); dreams/ lives at ${tier1Dir}/dreams, which is outside that watch when memoryPath is the workspace and inside it when memoryPath is <workspace>/memory. shouldIndexMemoryPath excludes only subagent-, root MEMORY.md and HEARTBEAT.md (memory-scanner.ts:44-57). The dream pass takes the 30 newest chunks by mtime: store.ts:2500-2504 'ORDER BY file_mtime_ms DESC, rowid DESC' via dream-pass.ts:230-233.
  Impact: Default-config agents never see their reflections anywhere; agents with memoryPath under memory/ feed the just-written dream log back as the newest 'recent memory chunks' on the next pass and into pre-turn retrieval, so the reflection daemon increasingly reflects on its own prior reflections.
  Fix: Exclude /dreams/ in shouldIndexMemoryPath, and if reflections are meant to be used, inject the latest themedReflection into the resume brief explicitly (bounded) instead of relying on the scanner.
- [medium/inert-config] **clawcode dream --priority is dropped by the daemon**
  Evidence: cli/commands/dream.ts:114-123 sends 'priority: args.priority === true,'. daemon.ts:7756-7770 builds the request from only agent, modelOverride, idleBypass and force; RunDreamPassRequest (daemon.ts:1456-1461) has no priority field; the IPC apply adapter is 'applyDreamResult: (agent, outcome) => dreamMachinery.applyDreamResult(agent, outcome),' (daemon.ts:7831-7832) so isPriorityPass defaults to false (dream-machinery.ts:436).
  Impact: The documented emergency override behaves exactly like a normal pass; an operator trying to force Row 5 cannot.
  Fix: Add priority to RunDreamPassRequest, parse it in the handler, and pass it as the third argument to dreamMachinery.applyDreamResult.
- [medium/inert-config] **dream.retentionDays is inert and every dream artifact grows without bound; the veto store re-reads its whole file every minute**
  Evidence: schema.ts:604 'retentionDays: z.number().int().min(1).max(365).optional(),' with the comment at :592-594 that the default is 'applied at the consumer (95-02 dream-log writer)'; dream-log-writer.ts contains no retention logic (only mkdir/readFile/writeFile, :157-189) and daemon.ts:10763 merely echoes 'retentionDays: cfg.dream.retentionDays ?? null'. No unlink of dreams/ exists in src/heartbeat. dream-veto-store.ts:180-204 readAllRows reads the entire JSONL, called from tick at :266-268 every minute (dream-veto-tick.ts:80 '*/${every} * * * *'); appendRow (:152-174) only appends. dream-graph-edges.ts:94-97 appends without a cap.
  Impact: dreams/*.md, graph-edges.json and dream-veto-pending.jsonl grow forever (each dream pass that names a candidate adds at least two veto rows); the per-minute tick becomes O(file) and the operator's retention setting does nothing.
  Fix: Add a heartbeat retention check modeled on src/heartbeat/checks/trace-retention.ts that deletes dreams/*.md older than retentionDays, and have the veto tick rewrite the JSONL keeping only pending rows plus the last N resolved ones.
- [medium/inert-config] **memory.consolidation.summaryModel is parsed but never read**
  Evidence: src/memory/schema.ts:25 'summaryModel: z.enum(["sonnet", "opus", "haiku"]).optional(),' and src/shared/types.ts:572. daemon.ts:4675-4683 builds consolidationConfig from enabled/weeklyThreshold/monthlyThreshold/schedule only and :4716 hard-codes 'summarize: (prompt: string) => summarizeWithHaiku(prompt, {}),' (also :15900); summarize-with-haiku.ts:50-55 calls callHaikuDirect, pinned to Haiku.
  Impact: Operators cannot raise digest quality for an important agent; weekly and monthly digests, the only long-horizon summaries, are always Haiku.
  Fix: Thread summaryModel into the daemon's summarize closure via callModelDirect, or delete the field.
- [medium/design-gap] **Dream cron config is frozen at boot although the config layer declares it hot-reloadable**
  Evidence: config/types.ts:160-166 lists '"agents.*.dream", "defaults.dream"' under RELOADABLE_FIELDS with the comment '(95-02 cron reads via the loader resolver each fire)'. daemon.ts:13324-13329 copies the values into a literal once: 'const dreamCfg = { enabled: agentCfg.dream?.enabled ?? false, idleMinutes: agentCfg.dream?.idleMinutes ?? 30, model: agentCfg.dream?.model ?? "haiku" };'. dream-cron.ts:196-198 returns '{ registered: false, reason: "disabled" }' and nothing registers later.
  Impact: Turning dreaming on, or changing idleMinutes, through a YAML edit reports a successful reload but has no effect until the daemon restarts; the operator believes the agent is dreaming.
  Fix: Register the cron for every agent and read resolvedAgents.find(...)?.dream inside onTick (skip when disabled), or mark the fields non-reloadable.
- [medium/data-loss] **The weekly digest can surface a consolidation digest as its top item and a dispute deletes it**
  Evidence: memory-digest.ts:25-30 DIGEST_EXCLUDE_TAGS is ['session-summary','mid-session','raw-fallback','confirmed'] with no weekly-digest/monthly-digest; selection sorts 'candidates.sort((a, b) => b.importance - a.importance ...' (:80). Consolidation inserts with importance 0.7/0.8 and tags ['weekly-digest', weekStr] (consolidation.ts:384-392, 439-447). Dispute path: memory-digest.ts:193-195 'const id = resolveMemoryIdByCode(store, item.code); ... const deleted = store.delete(id);'.
  Impact: A 0.7-importance digest outranks most saved memories, so item 1 of 'What I learned about you' is often the first prose line of the weekly digest; tapping that number deletes the whole week's searchable digest row (the .md file remains but is unembedded).
  Fix: Add 'weekly-digest' and 'monthly-digest' to DIGEST_EXCLUDE_TAGS (or exclude source === 'consolidation' in selectDigestMemories).
- [low/design-gap] **Weekly summarization truncates each day to the first ~4K chars, losing afternoons silently**
  Evidence: consolidation.ts:76 'const MAX_PROMPT_CHARS = 30000;' and :272-276 'const maxPerDay = Math.floor(MAX_PROMPT_CHARS / dailyLogs.length); if (content.length > maxPerDay) { content = content.slice(0, maxPerDay) + "\n\n[...truncated due to length]"; }'.
  Impact: For a 7-day week each daily log is cut at ~4,285 chars from the top, so anything decided late in a busy day never reaches the weekly digest, and the monthly digest inherits the gap. Raw detail survives only in the archived chunks.
  Fix: Summarize each day separately when a day exceeds its share, then summarize the day summaries (map-reduce), keeping the same section headers.
- [low/bug] **Digest selection caps by recency before sorting by importance**
  Evidence: memory-digest.ts:77 '.listInRange(options.since, options.until, 300)' and store.ts:584-586 'ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT ?', then the importance sort at memory-digest.ts:80.
  Impact: In a week with more than 300 memories the earliest high-importance saves are never candidates.
  Fix: Raise the limit or add an importance-ordered variant of listInRange.
- [low/bug] **Discord summary hard-codes '[auto-apply in 30m]' regardless of vetoWindowMinutes**
  Evidence: dream-discord-summary.ts:80-83 '`${target} [auto-apply in 30m]`' while the real deadline is computed from policy.vetoWindowMs (dream-machinery.ts:66; dream-auto-apply.ts:497).
  Impact: An operator with a 5-minute or 12-hour window is told 30 minutes and may miss the veto window.
  Fix: Pass vetoWindowMs into PromotionSummaryInput and render it.
- [low/bug] **Month grouping of weekly digests drifts by one day across the DST fall-back**
  Evidence: consolidation.ts:217-220 'const jan4 = new Date(isoYear, 0, 4); const jan4ISOWeekStart = startOfISOWeek(jan4); const weekStartDate = new Date(jan4ISOWeekStart.getTime() + (isoWeek - 1) * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);' (local midnight plus fixed 24h days).
  Impact: After the November transition the computed week start is Sunday 23:00 local; a week whose Monday is the 1st is filed under the previous month, so monthly digests can mix months or miss the threshold.
  Fix: Use date-fns setISOWeek/setISOWeekYear plus startOfISOWeek instead of millisecond arithmetic.
- [low/race] **A veto recorded while the tick is mid-apply is overwritten by the 'applied' row**
  Evidence: dream-veto-store.ts:272-291 reads rows once, runs applyFn, then appends '{ ...row, status: "applied", resolvedAt }' without re-checking; the veto IPC appends from a separate instance (daemon.ts:10789-10790 'const store = createDreamVetoStore(); await store.vetoRun(runId, reason);'); latestByRunId is last-write-wins (:210-218).
  Impact: A veto landing during the apply is lost: MEMORY.md already has the section and the row reads 'applied', so the operator's veto has no record.
  Fix: Re-read the latest status for row.runId before appending 'applied'; if it is 'vetoed', log a warning and append nothing.
- [low/dead-path] **Dead exports and unused parameters in the dream path**
  Evidence: findIdleAgents (idle-window-detector.ts:103-118) has no non-test caller; buildPromotionSummaryFromDream (dream-discord-summary.ts:127-146) has none; the CrossAgentCoordinator re-export (consolidation.ts:32-42) is referenced only by its own module and tests; DreamCronDeps.applyDreamResult is always shadowed because the daemon passes applyDreamResultPriority (daemon.ts:13359-13360; dream-cron.ts:258-264); ResolvedDreamConfig.retentionDays (dream-pass.ts:125) and DreamPromptInput.agentTz (dream-prompt-builder.ts:82, never passed at dream-pass.ts:245-251) are unused, so chunk timestamps render in host TZ.
  Impact: Misleading surface area; the cross-agent coordinator the comments promise for fleet-wide passes is unreachable, and operator-local timestamps never reach the dream prompt.
  Fix: Delete findIdleAgents, buildPromotionSummaryFromDream and the coordinator re-export; pass the agent TZ into buildDreamPrompt.
- [low/bug] **Dream log always records 'idle 0min'**
  Evidence: dream-auto-apply.ts:135 'idleMinutes: 0, // caller (cron tick) supplies actual; primitive doesn't recompute' and :431 'idleMinutes: 0,'; no caller supplies it (dream-machinery.ts:433-516 passes no idle value).
  Impact: The dream log header, the only human record of why a pass fired, misreports the trigger.
  Fix: Thread the isAgentIdle result (elapsed minutes and reason) from the cron tick through applyDreamResult into the entry.
- [low/performance] **All agents dream on the same minute phase**
  Evidence: dream-cron.ts:215 '`*/${stepMinutes} * * * *`' is identical for every agent with the same idleMinutes, registered in a loop at daemon.ts:13330; the dispatch goes through the Haiku bridge that error-guard.ts:17 notes 'has been failing ~240x/week'.
  Impact: A fleet of idle agents fires N concurrent one-shot calls at :00 and :30, raising the chance of bridge failures that land as 'failed' run-log rows.
  Fix: Offset the minute field by a stable hash of the agent name, or run dream passes through a fleet-wide queue of concurrency 1.
- [low/missing-feature] **No cross-agent consolidation or dream sharing exists despite the exported surface**
  Evidence: consolidation.ts:557-564 states 'the daemon's cron handler ... calls this function once PER agent ,  there is no fleet-level loop here' and points to CrossAgentCoordinator, which has no production caller; runDreamPass reads only the agent's own stores (dream-machinery.ts:252-253).
  Impact: Facts one agent learns never reach another agent's MEMORY.md or digests; the fleet has no shared long-term memory path.
  Fix: Out of scope for a small fix; at minimum remove the misleading re-export or add a single fleet-level call site behind a config flag.

**Strengths.**

- One dream implementation for both the manual IPC path and the cron (dream-machinery.ts), with a sentinel test (dream-cron-wiring.test.ts) that fails if daemon.ts stops registering the cron.
- Every outcome is a never-throw discriminated union and every attempt lands as one row in dream-runs.jsonl (dream-machinery.ts:355-431), so failures are visible in /clawcode-status; the reader is tail-bounded (dream-run-log.ts:130-177).
- The 32K prompt budget is actually enforced with graded degradation (chunks, then graph, then summaries, then MEMORY.md) and a final clamp (dream-prompt-builder.ts:296-347), with a byte-equivalence test.
- JSON extraction is brace-balanced and string-aware, surviving code fences and prose preambles (dream-pass.ts:267-298); schema failures are logged structurally.
- Veto rows snapshot the chunk text at schedule time so a scanner re-index between schedule and apply cannot lose a promotion (dream-auto-apply.ts:259-274; dream-veto-store.ts:44-52).
- promoteCandidates never duplicates, never pushes MEMORY.md past the injection cap, and leaves a provenance line naming the source path, run id and date (dream-promote.ts:83-92, 112-126).
- Consolidation never touches the in-progress ISO week, refuses empty or error-shaped summaries before archiving (error-guard.ts), archives only after the digest write, tolerates a closed store, and logs started/terminal rows.
- The veto tick is serialized with the approve IPC (dream-veto-tick.ts:53-78), apply failures mark rows expired with the error, and malformed JSONL lines are skipped rather than fatal.
- The weekly digest is stateless by design: item ids live in the message text, so reaction handling survives restarts (memory-digest.ts:10-15, 129-137).
- The cron step is clamped to a valid croner pattern and registration failure degrades one agent instead of crashing the daemon (dream-cron.ts:211-215, 282-293), with INC-2026-06-04 regression tests.

### Memory linking and graph (memory_links, GraphSearch, dream graph-edges), shared facts and cross-agent memory (SharedFactsStore, CrossAgentCoordinator, SharedDocRefStore, memory-roots), and the memory-pressure stack (memory-guard, memory-pressure, memory-imminence, memory-relief, memory-signal-bus, memory-census)

**How it works.** LINKING. Memories link through one adjacency table, memory_links(source_id, target_id, link_text, created_at) with PK (source,target) and ON DELETE CASCADE FKs to memories (src/memory/store.ts:1810-1824). Two producers exist. (1) Explicit wikilinks: extractWikilinks (src/memory/graph.ts:24-36) pulls [[target]] tokens; the target must be an existing memory id (store.ts:402-407 checks checkMemoryExists before insertLink; link_text is the target id). The same extraction runs on dedup-merge (store.ts:310-319, after deleteLinksFrom) and on cold re-import (src/memory/tier-manager.ts:282-292). (2) auto:similar edges: similarity.ts:181-182 writes a bidirectional pair for each KNN neighbour with cosine >= 0.6 (cold excluded, :164), eagerly after every insert/merge (store.ts:438, :330) and from the heartbeat cursor scan. No prompt or tool tells an agent to write [[<id>]], so in practice the graph is the auto:similar KNN graph. The dream pass produces a third kind of link, newWikilinks with from/to as file paths (dream-prompt-builder.ts:124), but those are appended to <memoryRoot>/graph-edges.json by appendDreamWikilinks (src/manager/dream-graph-edges.ts:50-99) and never enter memory_links.

DO LINKS INFLUENCE RETRIEVAL? Partly. GraphSearch.search (src/memory/graph-search.ts:54-173) runs SemanticSearch KNN, then for each hit pulls forward links and backlinks (:74-75), keeps neighbours with normalised cosine >= 0.3, caps 5 neighbours / 15 total, bumps their access_count (:123-126) and tags them source:"graph-neighbor" with linkedFrom. Its only production caller is the legacy branch of the memory-lookup IPC (src/manager/memory-lookup-handler.ts:170-198): scope="memories", page 0, no time window, which is exactly the default of the agent-facing memory_lookup MCP tool (src/mcp/server.ts:1588-1600). The per-turn pre-prompt retrieval (session-manager.ts:931-947 -> retrieveMemoryChunks) and the lazy clawcode_memory_search tool do not consult memory_links at all.
